CORPORATE PROFILE
China Mengniu Dairy Company Limited (the “Company”; stock code: 2319)
and its subsidiaries (collectively “the Group” or “Mengniu”) mainly
manufacture and distribute quality dairy products in China. It is one of the
leading dairy product manufacturers in China, with MENGNIU as its core
brand. Mengniu offers diversified products including liquid milk products, ice
cream, milk formula and cheese. In March 2014, Mengniu became a Hang
Seng Index constituent, making it the first blue-chip Chinese dairy product
manufacturer. In 2023, Mengniu’s MSCI ESG rating was elevated to AA.
Mengniu is a constituent of the Hang Seng Corporate Sustainability Index,
Hang Seng (Mainland and Hong Kong) Corporate Sustainability Index, Hang
Seng Corporate Sustainability Benchmark Index and HSI ESG Enhanced
Select Index.




公司簡介
中國蒙牛乳業有限公司（「本公司」；股份代號：2319）及其子公司（統稱「本
       ）主要於中國生產及銷售優質乳製品。憑藉其主要品牌蒙牛 ，
集團」或「蒙牛」
蒙牛已成為中國領先的乳製品生產商之一。蒙牛提供多元化的產品，包括
液態奶、冰淇淋、奶粉及奶酪產品。二零一四年三月，蒙牛獲納入恒生指
數成份股，成為首家中國乳製品企業藍籌股。二零二三年，蒙牛的MSCI ESG
評級升至AA級，並入選恒生可持續發展企業指數、恒生內地及香港可持續
發展企業指數、恒生可持續發展企業基準指數及恒指ESG增強精選指數。

      BUSINESS REVIEW                                                         業務回顧
      In the first half of 2024, the industry experienced a brief downturn    二零二四年上半年，原奶供給過剩，需求不及預
      due to an oversupply of raw milk, lower-than-expected consumer          期，需供矛盾凸顯，乳製品行業整體呈現短期下
      demand, and the imbalance between demand and supply. While              滑。蒙牛積極應對行業短期挑戰，堅定保持長期
      actively tackling short-term challenges and maintaining its             戰 略 定 力，錨 定「再 創 一 個 新 蒙 牛」戰 略 目 標 和
      long-term strategic focus, Mengniu has focused on its “Creating a       FIRST成功畫像，堅持「以價值創造為中心，持續
      New Mengniu” initiative and the FIRST blueprint. In promoting its       聚焦研發創新、品牌建設、數智化，加速渠道優
      overarching goal of high-quality development, it firmly adhered to      化和新業務發展」，推動高質量發展。
      the principles of “value creation, consistent focus on R&D and
      innovation, brand building, digitization, accelerated channel
      optimisation, and new business development”.

      During the reporting period, Mengniu consistently upgraded and         報告期內，蒙牛堅定品類創新升級，在極致品質
      innovated its product categories, created diverse new product          基礎上，進一步拓展豐富新品類、打造多元新場
      lines based on existing categories, and accelerated its expansion      景、加速To B渠道和專業乳品拓展，滿足消費者
      of To B channels to better meet the diverse and personalized           多樣化、個性化需求，引領乳製品從「喝牛奶」向
      consumer needs. In addition, Mengniu continued to lead the             「吃牛奶」，從「基礎供給」向「大健康」，從「粗加
      transformation and upgrade of dairy products from “drinking milk”      工」向「精深加工」的轉型升級。
      to “eating milk”, from “basic nutrition supply” to “greater health”
      and from “primary processing” to “value-added further
      processing”.

      Mengniu continued to focus on brand building, staying true to its       蒙牛持續聚焦品牌建設，傳承「天生要強」品牌精
      brand proposition of “born to excel”. As a member of the                神，作為奧林匹克全球合作夥伴(TOP)，期內圍繞
      Olympic Partners (TOP) programme, Mengniu further enhanced its          巴黎奧運會進一步推動品牌知名度、美譽度和全
      global brand awareness, reputation and influence by leveraging          球影響力全面提升。順應渠道重塑和變革趨勢，
      the platform of the Paris Olympics. In response to the trend of         蒙牛持續推動全渠道拓展和建設，保持旗下品牌
      channel restructuring and reform, Mengniu has actively promoted         在線下渠道既有優勢的同時，積極拓展高增的新
      omni-channel expansion and development. This includes actively          興渠道，構建線上線下全域融合的商業模式，多
      expanding high-growth emerging channels while preserving the            維度推動銷售轉化。
      strong presence of its brands in offline channels, thereby creating
      an integrated online and offline business model to drive sales in
      multiple dimensions.

      In response to the industry’s temporary demand and supply               針對行業階段性需供矛盾，蒙牛牢牢把握數智化
      imbalance, Mengniu has capitalized on the opportunity presented         轉型機遇，加速打造乳業新質生產力，對乳業鏈
      by digital transformation, accelerating the development of new,         條進行系統化、一體化重塑，全面激發奶業產業
      high quality production capabilities in the dairy industry and          鏈的效能潛力。同時，在運營層面，積極推動精
      undertaking a systematic and integrated transformation of the           益化管理，不斷打造扁平和高效組織，全面激活
      entire value chain. Meanwhile, at the operational level, Mengniu        組織效能，驅動公司運營效率和盈利能力的提升。
      has actively promoted precision management, and continues to
      build a lean and efficient organizational structure. These efforts
      are aimed at fully unlocking organizational potential and enhancing
      the Group’s operational efficiency and profitability.

OUTLOOK                                                                 展望
Driven by the Chinese government’s unwavering commitment to             在國家堅定實施擴大內需戰略、堅持高質量發展
policies aimed at expanding domestic demand and promote                 等政策推動下，宏觀經濟和居民消費信心有望持
high-quality development, the country’s macroeconomic                   續 回 暖。與 此 同 時，隨 著 國 民 健 康 意 識 不 斷 增
environment and consumer confidence are expected to continue            強，消費者對乳製品營養價值及健康功能更加重
recovering. Meanwhile, as consumers increasingly prioritize the         視，優 質、多 元 的 乳 製 品 將 持 續 受 到 消 費 者 青
nutritional value and health benefits of dairy products due to          睞，有 望 帶 來 新 的 消 費 增 長 點 和 結 構 性 升 級 機
growing awareness, premium and diversified offerings are set to         會，將驅動乳製品行業和營養健康產品的長期向
remain highly favoured. This trend is expected to create new            好發展。
opportunities for consumption growth and structural upgrades,
boosting the long-term development of the dairy industry as well
as nutrition and healthcare products.

As an industry leader, Mengniu is committed to tackling industry        作為行業領軍企業，蒙牛將積極應對行業挑戰，
challenges while remaining firmly committed to executing its            繼續堅定推動戰略落地執行，圍繞「以價值創造
strategic initiatives. The Group will adhere to the developmental       為中心，持續聚焦研發創新、品牌建設、數智化
direction of “value creation, consistent focus on R&D and               轉型，加速渠道優化和新業務發展」的發展方向，
innovation, brand building, digitization, accelerated channel           全面推動高質量發展，加快產品結構優化和業務
optimization and new business development”, with a strong               均衡發展，堅定推進渠道變革，強化內部精益化
emphasis on driving high-quality growth through accelerating the        運營管理、組織效能提升，持續打造消費者至愛
refinement of product mix and facilitating a balanced business          的、國際化的、更負責任的、擁有更強大文化基
expansion. The Group will continue to advance channel                   因的、數智化的FIRST牛，並引領中國奶業進入
transformation, enhance refined management of internal                  「高科技、高效能、高質量」的高質發展新階段。
operations, and further improve organizational efficiency. The
Group will continue to develop the “FIRST Mengniu” brand that is
beloved by consumers, into one that is increasingly international,
socially responsible, culturally strong and digitally transformed. We
aim to lead China’s dairy industry into a new stage of premium
development, characterized by “high-tech, high-efficiency, and
high-quality” growth.

      REPORT OF THE DIRECTORS
      董事會報告

      The board (the “Board”) of directors (the “Directors”) of the        本公司董事（「董事」）會（「董事會」）欣然提呈其中

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                        審閱範圍
We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong                   我們是按照香港會計師公會頒佈的《香港審閱委
Standard on Review Engagements 2410, Review of interim                 聘準則》第2410號「由實體的獨立核數師執行的中
financial information performed by the independent auditor of the      期財務資料審閱 」的規定進行審閱。中期財務報
entity , issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public         告的審閱主要包括向負責財務會計事宜的人士作
Accountants. A review of the interim financial report consists of      出詢問，並實施分析性及其他審閱程序。審閱的
making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial and   範 圍 遠 較 根 據《香 港 審 計 準 則》進 行 的 審 核 範 圍
accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review           小，故我們不能保證我們知悉一切可能於審核中
procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit      識別的重大事宜。因此，我們不發表審核意見。
conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing
and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that
we would become aware of all significant matters that might be
identified in an audit. Accordingly, we do not express an audit
opinion.

CONCLUSION                                                             結論
Based on our review, nothing has come to our attention that            根據我們的審閱，我們並未注意到有任何事項致
causes us to believe that the interim financial report as at 30        使我們相信截至二零二四年六月三十日的中期財
June 2024 is not prepared, in all material respects, in                務報告在所有重大方面並未按照國際會計準則第
accordance with International Accounting Standard 34, Interim          34號「中期財務報告 」及香港會計準則第34號「中
financial reporting , and Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34,            期財務報告 」編製。

      3   OPERATING SEGMENT INFORMATION                                     3      經營分部資料
          For management purposes, the Group is organised into                     出於管理需要，本集團按產品及服務構組
          business units based on their products and services and                  業務單元，並有以下五個可報告經營分部：
          has five reportable operating segments as follows:

          •     Liquid milk business — manufacture and distribution                •     液態奶業務 — 生產及經銷超高溫滅
                of ultra-high temperature milk (“UHT milk”), milk                        菌奶（「UHT奶」）、乳飲料、酸奶及鮮
                beverages, yogurt and fresh milk;                                        奶；

          •     Ice cream business — manufacture and distribution of               •     冰淇淋業務 — 生產及經銷含乳冰淇
                dairy-based ice cream;                                                   淋；

          •     Milk formula business — manufacture and distribution               •     奶粉業務 — 生產及經銷奶粉；
                of milk powder;

          •     Cheese business — manufacture and distribution of                  •     奶酪業務 — 生產及經銷奶酪；及
                cheese; and

          •     Others — principally the Group’s manufacture of raw                •     其他 — 主要為本集團乳製品原輔料
                materials for dairy products and trading business.                       生產及經銷貿易業務。

          Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating                 管理層獨立監察本集團各經營分部的業績，
          segments separately for the purpose of making decisions                  以作出有關資源分配及表現評估的決策。
          about resources allocation and performance assessment.                   分部表現乃根據可報告分部利潤╱虧損（其
          Segment performance is evaluated based on reportable                     為對經調整稅前利潤╱虧損的一種計量）予
          segment profit/loss, which is a measure of adjusted profit/              以評估。經調整稅前利潤╱虧損與本集團的
          loss before tax. The adjusted profit/loss before tax is                  稅前利潤╱虧損的計量方式一致，惟利息收
          measured consistently with the Group’s profit/loss before                入、非租賃相關融資成本、應佔聯營公司利
          tax except that interest income, non-lease-related finance               潤╱虧損、所得稅支出及總部及公司收益╱
          costs, share of profits/losses of associates, income tax                 支出不包含於該計量內。
          expense, as well as head office and corporate income/
          expenses are excluded from such measurement.

          Segment assets exclude investments in associates and                     分部資產並不包括於聯營公司的投資，以
          other unallocated head office and corporate assets as these              及其他不分部的總部及公司資產，乃由於
          assets are managed on a group basis.                                     此等資產按集團層面管理。

          Segment liabilities exclude liabilities of other unallocated             分部負債並不包括其他不分部的總部及公
          head office and corporate liabilities as these liabilities are           司負債，乃由於此等負債按集團層面管理。
          managed on a group basis.

          Intersegment sales and transfers are transacted with                     分部間銷售及轉讓乃參考以當時市價向第
          reference to the selling prices used for sales made to third             三方銷售所採用的售價進行交易。
          parties at the then prevailing market prices.
